### direct-es

```

```

### direct-zh

```

```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history for your own branch that hasn't been shared yet, like tidying up commits before opening a PR.
Merge when the branch is shared or public, or when you want to keep the record of when work was actually integrated.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接跑：`bun test test/foo.test.ts`。也可以只写文件名的一部分，比如 `bun test foo`，它会匹配路径里含这段文字的测试文件。

如果想只跑某个用例，加 `-t "用例名"`（或 `--test-name-pattern`），例如 `bun test foo.test.ts -t "返回空数组"`。
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to the database on disk before the change is applied to the data itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart so committed changes survive and half-finished ones are rolled back.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着改那个 compaction 的 PR，把锁粒度细化，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 按 review 里提到的锁粒度问题，把 compaction 相关的锁拆细，缩小持锁范围，避免不必要的串行化。
- 只改 review 指出的这部分，不动无关代码。
- 在当前分支上提交，保持 PR 为草稿。

完成标准：
- 相关测试和 CI 在改动后通过，并把通过的命令和结果写进报告。
- 报告里说明每处锁的改动前后范围。

不要做的：
- 不合并到主分支，不推送到新分支，不把草稿 PR 标记为可合并。
- 不改 review 之外的功能逻辑。

工作位置：原来那个 compaction PR 的仓库和分支。
```

```
<script>
function render(b) {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  if (!b || !b.trains || !b.trains.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">每天早上看到的票价会记在这里</div>';
    return;
  }
  var limit = b.threshold || 600;
  var cheap = b.trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < limit; });
  cnt.textContent = cheap.length + ' 班';
  var min = cheap.length ? Math.min.apply(null, cheap.map(function (t) { return t.price; })) : null;
  sub.textContent = (min !== null ? '最低 ' + min + ' 元' : '暂无 ' + limit + ' 元以下的班次') + ' · ' + (b.updated || '');
  list.innerHTML = b.trains.map(function (t) {
    var left = t.left != null ? '余 ' + t.left + ' 张' : '';
    var ok = t.price < limit;
    return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + t.no + ' ' + (left ? '<span class="k-row-sub">' + left + '</span>' : '') +
      '</div><div class="k-row-value" style="color:var(' + (ok ? '--d-good' : '--d-muted') + ')">' + t.price + ' 元</div></div>';
  }).join('');
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('board')).then(render);
ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get('board')).then(render); });
```

```
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
```

```
  <div class="k-sum-value k-none" id="total">还没记</div>

</div>

<button class="k-btn" id="addDay" style="margin-top:12px;width:100%">加一天</button>

  <div class="k-chips" id="chips" style="margin:10px 0"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>花了什么</label><input class="k-input" id="nm" placeholder="例如 午饭"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>多少钱</label><input class="k-num" id="am" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0.00"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="add" style="margin-top:10px;width:100%">记下</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const KEY='bj_trip_v1';
const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
const fmt=n=>'¥'+Number(n).toFixed(2);
const pad=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');
let state={days:[{d:'2026-11-24',items:[]}]};
let cur=0, armed=null;
function label(d){const [y,m,dd]=d.split('-').map(Number);return m+'月'+dd+'日';}
function nextDate(d){const t=new Date(d+'T00:00:00');t.setDate(t.getDate()+1);return t.ge … tElementById('days').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const r=e.target.closest('.k-row'); if(!r)return;
  const key=r.dataset.d+'-'+r.dataset.i;
  if(armed===key){state.days[+r.dataset.d].items.splice(+r.dataset.i,1);armed=null;save();}
  else{armed=key;render();}
});
document.getElementById('chips').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('[data-c]'); if(!b)return; cur=+b.dataset.c; render();
});
document.getElementById('addDay').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const last=state.days[state.days.length-1].d;
  state.days.push({d:nextDate(last),items:[]}); cur=state.days.length-1; save();
});
document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const nm=document.getElementById('nm').value.trim();
  const a=parseFloat(document.getElementById('am').value);
  if(!nm||!(a>0))return;
  state.days[cur].items.push({n:nm,a:Math.round(a*100)/100});
  document.getElementById('nm').value='';document.getElementById('am').value='';
  save();
});
ducter.onChange(()=>{const v=ducter.get(KEY);if(v&&v.days){state=v;}render();});
(function(){const v=ducter.get(KEY);if(v&&v.days)state=v;render();ducter.summary(count()?'共 '+count()+' 笔,合计 '+fmt(total()):'还没记');})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
```

```
  [an app card under the message: made "宝宝喝奶", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2962 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">今天已喝</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="tot"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">记录</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div><div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没记，点下面「记一笔」</div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary><div class="k-card">
<div class="k-field"><label for="tm">时间（留空即现在）</label><input class="k-input" id="tm" placeholder="06:30"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label for="ml">奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="120"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
</div></details>
</main>
<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const p = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const now = new Date();
const TODAY = now.getFullYear() + '-' + p(now.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(now.getDate());
const NOW_HM = p(now.getHours()) + ':' + p(now.getMinutes());
let feeds = [];
const todayFeeds = () => feeds.filter(f => f.d === TODAY).sort((a, b) => a.t.localeCompare(b.t));

function save() { ducter.set('feeds', feeds); }

function render() {
  const tf = todayFeeds();
  const tot = tf.reduce((s, f) => s + f.m … b.d + b.t).localeCompare(a.d + a.t))
    .forEach(f => {
      const row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      const main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = (f.d === TODAY ? '今天' : f.d.slice(5)) + ' ' + f.t;
      const val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = f.ml + ' ml';
      row.append(main, val);
      row.onclick = () => remove(f.i);
      list.append(row);
    });
}

function remove(i) {
  ducter.ask('删掉这一笔？').then(r => {
    if (r && r !== 'hold' && r !== 'no') {
      feeds.splice(i, 1);
      save();
      render();
    }
  });
}

$('add').onclick = () => {
  const raw = $('tm').value.trim() || NOW_HM;
  const ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(raw) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  feeds.push({ d: TODAY, t: raw.padStart(5, '0'), ml });
  $('tm').value = '';
  $('ml').value = '';
  save();
  render();
};

function sync(v) { feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : []; render(); }
function load() {
  const v = ducter.get('feeds');
  if (v && v.then) v.then(sync); else sync(v);
}
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-24","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-24","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<style>
.k-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;cursor:pointer}
.k-row input{width:20px;height:20px;accent-color:var(--d-accent);flex:none}
.k-row.is-landed .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-row-main{flex:1;min-width:0}
.k-row-sub{color:var(--d-muted);font-size:13px;margin-top:2px}
.k-row-value{font-size:12px;color:var(--d-soft);white-space:nowrap}
</style>
</main>
<script>
var ITEMS = ducter.get("items") || [];
var done = {};
function render(){
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  var left = 0;
  ITEMS.forEach(function(it){
    var on = !!done[it.id];
    if(!on) left++;
    var row = document.createElement('label');
    row.className = 'k-row' + (on ? ' is-landed' : '');
    var cb = document.createElement('input');
    cb.type = 'checkbox';
    cb.checked = on;
    cb.addEventListener('change', function(){
      done[it.id] = cb.checked;
      ducter.set('landed', done);
      render();
    });
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.innerHTML = '<div>' + it.title + '</div><div class="k-row-sub">' + it.sub + '</div>';
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = on ? 'landed' : it.status;
    row.appendChild(cb);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  document.getElementById('count').textContent = left;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = left === 0 ? 'All landed' : 'Tick each one off as it lands';
  ducter.summary(left + ' waiting');
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function(v){
  done = v || {};
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(function(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function(v){
    done = v || {};
    render();
  });
});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review","status":"since Mon"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing","status":"CI red"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice's review","status":"since Tue"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","sub":"Draft, not yet up for review","status":"draft"}]}
```

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我在 TTL 那个任务里补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

在现有的 TTL 假时钟测试基础上，再补一条测试，覆盖时间正好等于过期时刻（expiry 那一毫秒）的情况。要求：
- 时钟推进到恰好等于过期时间时，该值应视为已过期（或按现有约定的边界语义，并在测试名里写清楚）；时钟比过期时间少 1ms 时仍然有效。
- 继续用假时钟，不要用真实 sleep 或真实计时。
- 完成标准：跑新增和已有的 TTL 测试，全部通过，并把输出结果报告回来。
- 范围外：不合并、不推送、不开 PR；只在当前分支上提交。
```
